country_event = {

	title = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.1.t
	desc = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.1.d

		}

		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoEHaremUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_euroharem_menu_2	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_menu_2_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoEHaremUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_euroharem_menu_3	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_menu_3_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoEHaremUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_euroharem_menu_4	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_menu_4_upgrade
		}	
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_euroharem_menu_5	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_menu_5_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoEHaremUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}

			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_euroharem_menu_6

		value = 1
		}

		}
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_euroharem_menu_1

#Start event from estate
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.10
	title = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.10.t
	desc = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.10.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_EUROPE_MENU_6B_1_eventPicture

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		NOT = {
		has_ruler_flag = visiting_concubines
	}
			OR = {
				culture_group = germanic		
				culture_group = scandinavian		
				culture_group = british		
				culture_group = gaelic
				culture_group = latin
				culture_group = iberian		
				culture_group = french	
				culture_group = carpathian		
				culture_group = south_slavic		
				culture_group = west_slavic			
				culture_group = east_slavic		
				culture_group = baltic		
				culture_group = byzantine		
				culture_group = caucasian
			}	
	has_ruler_flag = cosmo_harem_launch
		
		}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		set_ruler_flag = visiting_concubines
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_harem_launch
		remove_estate_privilege= estate_harem_ruler_visit
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			}
			random_list = {
				33 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_adm
				}
				33 = { #dip
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_dip
	
				}
				33 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_mil
	
				}																				
			}
		}		
		}
		}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.10.a	
		if = {
			limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital #china
				has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_pirate_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = comso_ooku_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_purconcubines_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_eurowives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
			}
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.30
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital #china
				has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = comso_ooku_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_purconcubines_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_eurowives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
			}	
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.10100
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_piety_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.20
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_advisors_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_onsenaffairs_events.401
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_renovate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.1
			}
		}																										
	}					
}

#grant palace 1a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.11
	title = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.11.t
	desc = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.11.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_EUROPE_MENU_1AW_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = euro_concubine_1a
		}
		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = leonese
				culture = castillian
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = leonese
				culture = castillian

			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = leonese
				primary_culture = castillian					
				}
		}			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = euro_concubine_1a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = euro_concubine_1a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = leonese
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = leonese_consort

		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = aragonese
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = aragonese_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = leonese_consort

		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = castillian
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = castillian_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = aragonese_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = leonese_consort

		}												
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = leonese_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = leonese
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = leonese
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = leonese
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = aragonese_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = aragonese
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = aragonese
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = aragonese
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = castillian_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = castillian
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = castillian
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = castillian
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}												
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = euro_concubine_1a
				duration =  -1
				desc = euro_concubine_1a_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.11.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
			OR = {
				primary_culture = leonese
				primary_culture = aragonese
				primary_culture = castillian
			}		

					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = leonese_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = leonese
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = aragonese_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = aragonese
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = castillian_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = castillian
		}
		}												
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_euroharem_1a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_eumarriage_events.20
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = oirats_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = uzbehk_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = turkmeni_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = khalkha_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = chahar_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = mongol_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = leonese_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = aragonese_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = castillian_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.11.b
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}								
		clr_ruler_flag = oirats_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = uzbehk_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = turkmeni_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = khalkha_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = chahar_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = mongol_consort				
		clr_ruler_flag = leonese_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = aragonese_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = castillian_consort
		}					
}

#grant harem 1b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.12
	title = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.12.t
	desc = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.12.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_EUROPE_MENU_1B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = euro_concubine_1b
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 2
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = andalucian
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 2
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = andalucian
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = andalucian
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = euro_concubine_1b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = euro_concubine_1b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = andalucian
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = andalucian
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = andalucian
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10

#grant harem 1c
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.13
	title = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.13.t
	desc = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.13.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_EUROPE_MENU_1C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = euro_concubine_1c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 2
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = portugese
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 2
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = portugese
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = portugese
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = euro_concubine_1c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = euro_concubine_1c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = portugese
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = portugese
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = portugese
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = euro_concubine_1c
				duration =  -1
				desc = euro_concubine_1c_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}

					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = greek
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_euroharem_2b								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_eumarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}

		tooltip = {

#grant harem 2c
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.23
	title = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.23.t
	desc = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.23.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_EUROPE_MENU_2C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = euro_concubine_2c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 10
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = albanian
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 10
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = albanian
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = albanian
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = euro_concubine_2c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = euro_concubine_2c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = albanian
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = albanian
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = albanian
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = euro_concubine_2c
				duration =  -1
				desc = euro_concubine_2c_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.23.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = albanian
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = albanian
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_euroharem_2c								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_eumarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.23.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650

		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

#grant harem 3c
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.33
	title = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.33.t
	desc = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.33.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_EUROPE_MENU_3C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = euro_concubine_3c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = hungarian
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = hungarian
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = hungarian
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = euro_concubine_3c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = euro_concubine_3c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hungarian
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hungarian
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hungarian
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = euro_concubine_3c
				duration =  -1
				desc = euro_concubine_3c_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.33.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = hungarian
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = hungarian
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_euroharem_3c								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_eumarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.33.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = euro_concubine_4c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = slovak
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = slovak
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = slovak
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = euro_concubine_4c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = euro_concubine_4c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = slovak
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = slovak
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = slovak
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = euro_concubine_4c
				duration =  -1
				desc = euro_concubine_4c_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.43.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = slovak
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = slovak
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_euroharem_4c								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_eumarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {

			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace

			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}

		tooltip = {

			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace

#grant harem 6a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.61
	title = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.61.t
	desc = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.61.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_EUROPE_MENU_6A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = euro_concubine_6a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = novgorodian
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = novgorodian
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = novgorodian
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = euro_concubine_6a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = euro_concubine_6a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = novgorodian
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = novgorodian
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = novgorodian
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.71
	title = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.71.t
	desc = cosmo_euroharemestate_events.71.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_EUROPE_MENU_7A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = euro_concubine_7a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = uralic
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = uralic
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = uralic
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = euro_concubine_7a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = euro_concubine_7a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = uralic
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = uralic
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = uralic
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = euro_concubine_7a
				duration =  -1
				desc = euro_concubine_7a_DESC
				}			
			}

country_event = {

		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoEHaremThreeBCounter	
			value = 400
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_euroese_army_organizer
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoEHaremThreeBCounter	
			value = 400
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoEHaremThreeBCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}	
	}

					}
				}

						has_province_modifier = euro_concubine_11b
						has_province_modifier = euro_concubine_11c
					}
				}

				any_province = {

						has_province_modifier = euro_concubine_12b

				}
				}
			country_event = {

			}
			}

			}
		}
	}		   		
	}
